\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_training_data\\\\\\\"\\\\nOUT_PATH = \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/artifacts/bm25_overlap_stratified.json\\\\\\\"\\\\nSTRATEGIES = [\\\\\\\"random\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"embedding_distance\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"dataflow_aware\\\\\\\"]\\\\nSEED = 42\\\\nK = 7\\\\nTIERS = {\\\\n    \\\\\\\"small_lt50\\\\\\\": (0, 50),\\\\n    \\\\\\\"medium_50_200\\\\\\\": (50, 201),\\\\n    \\\\\\\"large_200plus\\\\\\\": (201, float(\\\\\\\"inf\\\\\\\")),\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef extract_repo(query_id: str) -> str:\\\\n    iid = 1)[1]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef jaccard(a: set, b: set) -> float:\\\\n    if not a and not b:\\\\n        return 1.0\\\\n    union = a | b\\\\n    if not union:\\\\n        return 0.0\\\\n    return len(a & b) / len(union)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_strategy(name: str) -> dict:\\\\n    path = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, f\\\\\\\"train_{name}_s{SEED}.jsonl\\\\\\\")\\\\n    data = {}\\\\n    with open(path) as Build repo file count (union of all file paths across all strategies)\\\\n    repo_files = defaultdict(set)\\\\n    for qid, negs in bm25_data.items():\\\\n        repo = extract_repo(qid)\\\\n        repo_files[repo].add(extract_filepath(qid))\\\\n        for nc in negs:\\\\n            repo_files[repo].add(extract_filepath(nc))\\\\n    for strat in STRATEGIES:\\\\n        for qid, negs in other_data[strat].items():\\\\n            repo = extract_repo(qid)\\\\n            repo_files[repo].add(extract_filepath(qid))\\\\n            for nc in negs:\\\\n                repo_files[repo].add(extract_filepath(nc))\\\\n\\\\n    repo_size = {repo: len(files) for repo, files in repo_files.items()}\\\\n\\\\n    # 3. \\\\\\\"repo_count\\\\\\\": len(repos_in_tier),\\\\n            \\\\\\\"instance_count\\\\\\\": len(tier_records),\\\\n        }\\\\n        for strat in STRATEGIES:\\\\n            key = stats[f\\\\\\\"avg_overlap_bm25_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\\\\\"] = round(float(arr.mean()), 4)\\\\n                stats[f\\\\\\\"median_overlap_bm25_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\\\\\"] = round(float(np.median(arr)), 4)\\\\n                stats[f\\\\\\\"matched_queries_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\\\\\"] = len(vals)\\\\n            else:\\\\n                stats[f\\\\\\\"avg_overlap_bm25_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\\\\\"] = None\\\\n stats[f\\\\\\\"matched_queries_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\\\\\"] = 0\\\\n        return stats\\\\n\\\\n    result = {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"overall\\\\\\\": embedding_distance, dataflow_aware),\\n# stratified by repo size (unique file count).\\n#\\n# Input:  /root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_training_data/train_{strategy}_s42.jsonl\\n# Output: /root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/artifacts/bm25_overlap_stratified.json\\n\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nDATA_DIR = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_training_data\\\"\\nOUT_PATH = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/artifacts/bm25_overlap_stratified.json\\\"\\nSTRATEGIES = [\\\"random\\\", \\\"embedding_distance\\\", \\\"dataflow_aware\\\"]\\nSEED = 42\\nK = 7\\nTIERS = {\\n    \\\"small_lt50\\\": (0, 50),\\n    \\\"medium_50_200\\\": (50, 201),\\n    \\\"large_200plus\\\": (201, float(\\\"inf\\\")),\\n}\\n\\n\\ndef extract_repo(query_id: str) -> str:\\n    iid = query_id.split(\\\"::\\\")[0]\\n    return iid.split(\\\".\\\")[0]\\n\\n\\ndef extract_filepath(full_id: str) -> str:\\n    return full_id.split(\\\"::\\\", 1)[1]\\n\\n\\ndef jaccard(a: set, b: set) -> float:\\n    if not a and not b:\\n        return 1.0\\n    union = a | b\\n    if not union:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return len(a & b) / len(union)\\n\\n\\ndef load_strategy(name: str) -> dict:\\n    path = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, f\\\"train_{name}_s{SEED}.jsonl\\\")\\n    data = {}\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            d = json.loads(line)\\n            data[d[\\\"query_id\\\"]] = d[\\\"neg_candidates\\\"]\\n    return data\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    # 1. Load all strategies\\n    bm25_data = load_strategy(\\\"bm25\\\")\\n    other_data = {s: load_strategy(s) for s in STRATEGIES}\\n\\n    # 2. Build repo file count (union of all file paths across all strategies)\\n    repo_files = defaultdict(set)\\n    for qid, negs in bm25_data.items():\\n        repo = extract_repo(qid)\\n        repo_files[repo].add(extract_filepath(qid))\\n        for nc in negs:\\n            repo_files[repo].add(extract_filepath(nc))\\n    for strat in STRATEGIES:\\n        for qid, negs in other_data[strat].items():\\n            repo = extract_repo(qid)\\n            repo_files[repo].add(extract_filepath(qid))\\n            for nc in negs:\\n                repo_files[repo].add(extract_filepath(nc))\\n\\n    repo_size = {repo: len(files) for repo, files in repo_files.items()}\\n\\n    # 3. Compute per-query overlaps (only for queries present in both BM25 and other)\\n    records = []  # list of dicts with overlap info per query\\n    for qid, bm25_negs in bm25_data.items():\\n        repo = extract_repo(qid)\\n        rsize = repo_size.get(repo, 0)\\n        bm25_topk = set(bm25_negs[:K])\\n\\n        row = {\\n            \\\"query_id\\\": qid,\\n            \\\"repo\\\": repo,\\n            \\\"repo_size\\\": rsize,\\n            \\\"bm25_pool_size\\\": len(bm25_negs),\\n        }\\n        for strat in STRATEGIES:\\n            if qid in other_data[strat]:\\n                other_topk = set(other_data[strat][qid][:K])\\n                row[f\\\"overlap_{strat}\\\"] = jaccard(bm25_topk, other_topk)\\n            else:\\n                row[f\\\"overlap_{strat}\\\"] = None\\n        records.append(row)\\n\\n    # 4. Stratify and compute stats\\n    def compute_tier_stats(tier_records):\\n        repos_in_tier = set(r[\\\"repo\\\"] for r in tier_records)\\n        stats = {\\n            \\\"repo_count\\\": len(repos_in_tier),\\n            \\\"instance_count\\\": len(tier_records),\\n        }\\n        for strat in STRATEGIES:\\n            key = f\\\"overlap_{strat}\\\"\\n            vals = [r[key] for r in tier_records if r[key] is not None]\\n            if vals:\\n                arr = np.array(vals)\\n                stats[f\\\"avg_overlap_bm25_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\"] = round(float(arr.mean()), 4)\\n                stats[f\\\"median_overlap_bm25_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\"] = round(float(np.median(arr)), 4)\\n                stats[f\\\"matched_queries_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\"] = len(vals)\\n            else:\\n                stats[f\\\"avg_overlap_bm25_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\"] = None\\n                stats[f\\\"median_overlap_bm25_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\"] = None\\n                stats[f\\\"matched_queries_{strat.replace('embedding_distance','embedding')}\\\"] = 0\\n        return stats\\n\\n    result = {\\n        \\\"overall\\\": compute_tier_stats(records),\\n        \\\"seed\\\": f\\\"s{SEED} only\\\",\\n        \\\"k\\\": K,\\n        \\\"repo_size_method\\\": \\\"union of unique file paths across all strategies\\\",\\n    }\\n\\n    for tier_name, (lo, hi) in TIERS.items():\\n        tier_records = [r for r in records if lo <= r[\\\"repo_size\\\"] < hi]\\n        result[tier_name] = compute_tier_stats(tier_records)\\n        # Add repo size stats for this tier\\n        sizes = [r[\\\"repo_size\\\"] for r in tier_records]\\n        if sizes:\\n            result[tier_name][\\\"avg_repo_size\\\"] = round(float(np.mean(sizes)), 1)\\n            result[tier_name][\\\"min_repo_size\\\"] = int(min(sizes))\\n            result[tier_name][\\\"max_repo_size\\\"] = int(max(sizes))\\n\\n    # Add per-tier BM25 pool size stats (how many BM25 candidates available)\\n    for tier_name, (lo, hi) in TIERS.items():\\n        tier_records = [r for r in records if lo <= r[\\\"repo_size\\\"] < hi]\\n        pools = [r[\\\"bm25_pool_size\\\"] for r in tier_records]\\n        if pools:\\n            result[tier_name][\\\"avg_bm25_pool_size\\\"] = round(float(np.mean(pools)), 1)\\n\\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(OUT_PATH), exist_ok=True)\\n    with open(OUT_PATH, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(result, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n\\n    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}}}

\\\"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Stratified analysis of embedding vs random negative strategies by data strata.\\\\n\\\\nAnalyses M2 per-instance predictions across:\\\\n  - Repo size (instance count per repo from M1)\\\\n  - Candidate pool size (neg_candidates per query)\\\\n  - Query-positive similarity (cosine sim from model, or text overlap for null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.39}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\ndef mock_predictions(\\n    val_samples: List[Dict], strategy: str, seed: int\\n) -> List[Dict]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + abs(hash(strategy)) % 10000)\\n    results = []\\n    for sample in val_samples:\\n        pool = 1 + sample[\\\"pool_size\\\"]\\n        if strategy == \\\"random\\\":\\n            rank = rng.randint(1, pool + 1)\\n        elif strategy == \\\"embedding_distance\\\":\\n            rank = max(1, min(pool, int(rng.exponential(pool * 0.35)) + 1))\\n        else:\\n            rank = max(1, min(pool, int(rng.exponential(pool * 0.20)) + 1))\\n\\n        pos_sim = max(0.0, min(1.0, 1.0 - rank / pool + rng.normal(0, 0.08)))\\n        tsim = text_similarity(\\n            sample[\\\"query_tokens\\\"][:500], sample[\\\"pos_tokens\\\"][:500]\\n        )\\n\\n        results.append({\\n            \\\"query_id\\\": sample[\\\"query_id\\\"],\\n            \\\"instance_id\\\": sample[\\\"instance_id\\\"],\\n            \\\"pool_size\\\": sample[\\\"pool_size\\\"],\\n            \\\"rank\\\": rank,\\n            \\\"pos_sim\\\": pos_sim if pos_sim > 0 else tsim,\\n            \\\"total_candidates\\\": pool,\\n        })\\n    return results\\n\\n\\n# ── Metrics computation ──────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef compute_metrics(results: List[Dict]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n    if not results:\\n        return {\\\"R@1\\\": 0.0, \\\"R@5\\\": 0.0, \\\"MRR\\\": 0.0, \\\"count\\\": 0}\\n    ranks = [r[\\\"rank\\\"] for r in results]\\n    n = len(ranks)\\n    return {\\n        \\\"R@1\\\": sum(1 for r in ranks if r <= 1) / n,\\n        \\\"R@5\\\": sum(1 for r in ranks if r <= 5) / n,\\n        \\\"MRR\\\": sum(1.0 / r for r in ranks) / n,\\n        \\\"count\\\": n,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef _metric_fn(results, metric_name):\\n    m = compute_metrics(results)\\n    return m[metric_name]\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_ci(\\n    results: List[Dict],\\n    metric_name: str = \\\"R@1\\\",\\n    n_bootstrap: int = 1000,\\n    ci: float = 0.95,\\n    seed: int = 42,\\n) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n = len(results)\\n    if n == 0:\\n        return (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)\\n\\n    point = _metric_fn(results, metric_name)\\n    boot_values = []\\n    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\\n        idx = rng.choice(n, n, replace=True)\\n        boot_sample = [results[i] for i in idx]\\n        boot_values.append(_metric_fn(boot_sample, metric_name))\\n\\n    alpha = (1 - ci) / 2\\n    lo = float(np.percentile(boot_values, alpha * 100))\\n    hi = float(np.percentile(boot_values, (1 - alpha) * 100))\\n    return (point, lo, hi)\\n\\n\\n# ── Stratification ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef assign_strata(\\n    results: List[Dict],\\n    inst_to_repo: Dict[str, str],\\n    repo_counts: Dict[str, int],\\n) -> List[Dict]:\\n    for r in results:\\n        repo = inst_to_repo.get(r[\\\"instance_id\\\"], \\\"\\\")\\n        rc = repo_counts.get(repo, 0)\\n        r[\\\"repo\\\"] = repo\\n        r[\\\"repo_size\\\"] = rc\\n        r[\\\"repo_size_bin\\\"] = _bin_value(rc, REPO_SIZE_BINS)\\n        r[\\\"pool_size_bin\\\"] = _bin_value(r[\\\"pool_size\\\"], POOL_SIZE_BINS)\\n        r[\\\"sim_bin\\\"] = _bin_value(r[\\\"pos_sim\\\"], SIMILARITY_BINS)\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef group_by(results: List[Dict], key: str) -> Dict[str, List[Dict]]:\\n    groups: Dict[str, List[Dict]] = defaultdict(list)\\n    for r in results:\\n        groups[r[key]].append(r)\\n    return dict(groups)\\n\\n\\n# ── Analysis core ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef run_stratified_analysis(\\n    all_results: Dict[str, Dict[int, List[Dict]]],\\n    strata_key: str,\\n    bin_order: List[str],\\n    n_bootstrap: int = 1000,\\n) -> Dict:\\n    analysis = {}\\n    for bin_name in bin_order:\\n        analysis[bin_name] = {}\\n        for strategy in STRATEGIES:\\n            seed_results = []\\n        metrics = compute_metrics(seed_results)\\n            ci_r1 = bootstrap_ci(seed_results, \\\"R@1\\\", n_bootstrap)\\n            ci_r5 = bootstrap_ci(seed_results, \\\"R@5\\\", n_bootstrap)\\n            ci_mrr = bootstrap_ci(seed_results, \\\"MRR\\\", n_bootstrap)\\n\\n            analysis[bin_name][strategy] = \\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 12890 字符] ...\\n\\n file not found: {val_file}, skipping\\\", flush=True)\\n                continue\\n\\n            if args.dry_run:\\n                print(f\\\"  Generating mock predictions: {strategy} seed={seed}\\\", flush=True)\\n                results = mock_predictions(val_samples, strategy, seed)\\n            elif args.predictions_dir:\\n                pred_pattern = os.path.join(\\n                    args.predictions_dir, f\\\"*{strategy}*s{seed}*.json\\\"\\n                )\\n                import glob\\n                pred_files = glob.glob(pred_pattern)\\n                if pred_files:\\n                    print(f\\\"  Loading predictions: {pred_files[0]}\\\", flush=True)\\n                    results = load_predictions(pred_files[0], val_samples)\\n                else:\\n                    print(f\\\"  [WARN] No prediction file matching {pred_pattern}\\\", flush=True)\\n                    continue\\n            elif args.checkpoints_dir:\\n                ckpt_dir = os.path.join(\\n                    args.checkpoints_dir, f\\\"{strategy}_s{seed}\\\", \\\"best\\\"\\n                )\\n                if not os.path.exists(ckpt_dir):\\n                    ckpt_dir = os.path.join(args.checkpoints_dir, f\\\"{strategy}_s{seed}\\\")\\n                if os.path.exists(ckpt_dir):\\n                    print(f\\\"  Re-evaluating checkpoint: {ckpt_dir}\\\", flush=True)\\n                    results = reeval_checkpoint(\\n                        ckpt_dir, val_samples, args.device, args.max_seq_length\\n                    )\\n                else:\\n                    print(f\\\"  [WARN] Checkpoint not found: {ckpt_dir}\\\", flush=True)\\n                    continue\\n            else:\\n                print(\\\"  [ERROR] No --dry-run, --predictions-dir, or --checkpoints-dir\\\", flush=True)\\n                continue\\n\\n            # Assign strata\\n            results = assign_strata(results, inst_to_repo, repo_counts)\\n\\n            # For dry-run or model-based: compute text similarity for sim_bin\\n            if args.dry_run:\\n                for r, s in zip(results, val_samples):\\n                    tsim = text_similarity(s[\\\"query_tokens\\\"][:500], s[\\\"pos_tokens\\\"][:500])\\n                    r[\\\"sim_bin\\\"] = _bin_value(tsim, SIMILARITY_BINS)\\n\\n            all_results[strategy][seed] = results\\n            print(f\\\"  -> {len(results)} instances\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n    # Run stratified analysis\\n    print(\\\"\\\\nRunning stratified analysis...\\\", flush=True)\\n    repo_analysis = run_stratified_analysis(\\n        all_results, \\\"repo_size_bin\\\", list(REPO_SIZE_BINS.keys()), args.n_bootstrap\\n    )\\n    pool_analysis = run_stratified_analysis(\\n        all_results, \\\"pool_size_bin\\\", list(POOL_SIZE_BINS.keys()), args.n_bootstrap\\n    )\\n    sim_analysis = run_stratified_analysis(\\n        all_results, \\\"sim_bin\\\", list(SIMILARITY_BINS.keys()), args.n_bootstrap\\n    )\\n\\n    # Save JSON\\n    full_results = {\\n        \\\"config\\\": {\\n            \\\"strategies\\\": STRATEGIES,\\n            \\\"seeds\\\": seeds,\\n            \\\"repo_size_bins\\\": {k: list(v) for k, v in REPO_SIZE_BINS.items()},\\n            \\\"pool_size_bins\\\": {k: list(v) for k, v in POOL_SIZE_BINS.items()},\\n            \\\"similarity_bins\\\": {k: list(v) for k, v in SIMILARITY_BINS.items()},\\n            \\\"n_bootstrap\\\": args.n_bootstrap,\\n            \\\"dry_run\\\": args.dry_run,\\n        },\\n        \\\"by_repo_size\\\": repo_analysis,\\n        \\\"by_pool_size\\\": pool_analysis,\\n        \\\"by_similarity\\\": sim_analysis,\\n    }\\n\\n    json_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \\\"stratified_results.json\\\")\\n    with open(json_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(full_results, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n    print(f\\\"  Results JSON saved to {json_path}\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n    # CSV summary\\n    csv_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \\\"stratified_summary.csv\\\")\\n    write_summary_csv(repo_analysis, pool_analysis, sim_analysis, csv_path)\\n\\n    # Plots\\n    try:\\n        make_plots(repo_analysis, pool_analysis, sim_analysis, args.output_dir)\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        print(f\\\"  [WARN] Plot generation failed: {e}\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n    print(\\\"\\\\nDone.\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"}}}
